  function PrepareRagdollGrab()

    local val157 = scriptRagdollGrabToy or isRagdollGrabRespawning
local val158

    if val157 then
      return
    else
      isRagdollGrabRespawning = true

      for index21, value59 in ipairs(waitForChild:GetChildren()) do
        local element7 = value59

        if element7.Name == "RagdollGrab" then
          pcall(function()

            replicatedStorage.MenuToys.DestroyToy:FireServer(element7)
            return
          end)
        end
      end

      local generateGUID = HttpService:GenerateGUID(false)
      spawnToyRemoteFunction2 = replicatedStorage.MenuToys.SpawnToyRemoteFunction

      for n = 1, 3 do
        local character31 = val.Character
        local head2 = character31

        if character31 then

          head2 = val.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
        end

        local element8 = head2

        if not element8 then
          task.wait(0.5)
          
        else
          task.spawn(function()
            spawnToyRemoteFunction2:InvokeServer("PalletLightBrown", (CFrame.new(
              element8.Position + (Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)), element8.Position
            )) * (CFrame.Angles(math.pi, 0, 0)), Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))

            return
          end)

          local connect31
          local val159

          connect31 = waitForChild.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child3)
            if child3.Name == "PalletLightBrown" then
              val159 = child3
            end

            return
          end)

          local val160 = tick()

          if not val159 then
            tick()
          end

          repeat
            task.wait()

            val158 = val159 or (tick()) - val160 > 8
          until val158

          connect31:Disconnect()
          local ragdollGrab = val159

          if ragdollGrab then
            if (ragdollGrab:FindFirstChild("ScriptRagdollID")) then

              ragdollGrab.ScriptRagdollID:Destroy()
            end

            local scriptRagdollID = Instance.new("StringValue")
            scriptRagdollID.Name = "ScriptRagdollID"
            scriptRagdollID.Value = generateGUID
            scriptRagdollID.Parent = ragdollGrab

            ragdollGrab.Name = "RagdollGrab"
            local waitForChild6 = ragdollGrab:WaitForChild("SoundPart", 5)

            if waitForChild6 then
              task.spawn(function()

                while true do
                  local ragdollGrab2 = RagdollGrab
                  local parent = ragdollGrab2

                  if ragdollGrab2 then

                    parent = waitForChild6 and waitForChild6.Parent
                  end

                  if parent then

                    if not (waitForChild6:FindFirstChild("PartOwner"))
                      or waitForChild6.PartOwner.Value ~= val.Name then

                      replicatedStorage.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(
                        waitForChild6, waitForChild6.CFrame
                      )
                    end

                    task.wait(0.1)
                  else
                    break
                  end
                end

                return
              end)

              local bodyVelocity4 = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
              bodyVelocity4.Parent = waitForChild6
              bodyVelocity4.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0)
              bodyVelocity4.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 1000, 0)

              for key5, value60 in pairs(ragdollGrab:GetChildren()) do
                if (value60:IsA("BasePart")) then
                  value60.Transparency = 1
                  value60.Size = Vector3.new(0.5, 0.5, 0.5)
                  value60.CanCollide = false
                end
              end

              if (waitForChild6:FindFirstChild("Wood")) then

                waitForChild6.Wood:Destroy()
              end

              scriptRagdollGrabToy = ragdollGrab
              RAGDOLLGRAB_UNIQUE_ID = generateGUID
              isRagdollGrabRespawning = false
              UpdateRagdollGrabPalletMenu()
              return
            else
              ragdollGrab:Destroy()
              
              task.wait(0.5)
              
            end
          else
            task.wait(0.5)
          end
        end
      end

      isRagdollGrabRespawning = false
      return
    end
  end

  function DestroyRagdollGrab()

    return
  end

  end

  function anchorGrab()
    while true do
      pcall(function()

        local grabParts6 = workspace:FindFirstChild("GrabParts")
local val196

        if not grabParts6 then
          return
        else
          local grabPart9 = grabParts6:FindFirstChild("GrabPart")

          if not grabPart9 then
            return
          else
            local weldConstraint9 = grabPart9:FindFirstChild("WeldConstraint")

            if not weldConstraint9 or not weldConstraint9.Part1 then
              return
            else

              local part14 = weldConstraint9.Part1.Name == "SoundPart" and weldConstraint9.Part1
              local val197 = part14

              if not part14 then

                local soundPart4 = weldConstraint9.Part1.Parent:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")
                local primaryPart2 = soundPart4

                if not soundPart4 then

                  primaryPart2 = weldConstraint9.Part1.Parent.PrimaryPart
                    or weldConstraint9.Part1
                end

                val197 = primaryPart2
              end

              val196 = val197
              local val198 = not val196
              local val199 = val198

              if not val198 then
                local map = workspace.Map
                local isDescendantOf = map

                if map then
                  isDescendantOf = val196:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Map)
                end

                val199 = isDescendantOf
              end

              if val199 then
                return
              else
                local val200 = false

                for index25, value77 in ipairs(compiledGroups) do
                  if value77.primaryPart == val196 then
                    val200 = true
                    break
                  else
                    for index26, value78 in ipairs(value77.group) do
                      if value78.part == val196 then
                        val200 = true
                        break
                      end
                    end

                    if val200 then
                      break
                    end
                  end
                end

                if val200 then
                  return
                else
                  for key9, value79 in pairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if (val196:IsDescendantOf(value79.Character)) then
                      return
                    end
                  end

                  local val201 = true

                  for key10, value80 in pairs(val196:GetDescendants()) do
                    if (table.find(anchoredParts, value80)) then
                      val201 = false
                      break
                    end
                  end

                  if val201 and not (table.find(anchoredParts, val196)) then

                    local element14 = (FindFirstAncestorOfType(val196, "Model")) or val196
                    createHighlight(element14)
                    table.insert(anchoredParts, val196)

                    element14.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant2)
                      onPartOwnerChanged(descendant2, val196)
                      return
                    end)

                    for index27, value81 in ipairs(element14:GetDescendants()) do
                      onPartOwnerChanged(value81, val196)
                    end
                  end

                  local object5 = FindFirstAncestorOfType(val196, "Model")

                  if object5 and object5 ~= workspace then
                    for index28, value82 in ipairs(object5:GetDescendants()) do
                      local bodyPosition3 = value82:IsA("BodyPosition")

                      if bodyPosition3 or value82:IsA("BodyGyro") then
                        value82:Destroy()
                      end
                    end
                  else
                    for index29, value83 in ipairs(val196:GetChildren()) do
                      local bodyPosition4 = value83:IsA("BodyPosition")

                      if bodyPosition4 or value83:IsA("BodyGyro") then
                        value83:Destroy()
                      end
                    end
                  end

                  while true do

                    if not (workspace:FindFirstChild("GrabParts")) then
                      break
                    end

                    task.wait()
                  end

                  createBodyMovers(val196, val196.Position, val196.CFrame)
                  return
                end
              end
            end
          end
        end
      end)

      task.wait()
    end
  end

  function cleanupAnchoredParts()

    for index30, value84 in ipairs(anchoredParts) do

      if value84 and value84.Parent then
        if (value84:FindFirstChild("BodyPosition")) then

          value84.BodyPosition:Destroy()
        end

        if (value84:FindFirstChild("BodyGyro")) then

          value84.BodyGyro:Destroy()
        end

        local target = getHighlight(value84)

        if target then
          target:Destroy()
        end
      end
    end

    iterate4(anchoredConnections)
    return
  end

  function updateBodyMovers(val202)

    for index31, value85 in ipairs(compiledGroups) do
      if value85.primaryPart == val202 then
        for index32, value86 in ipairs(value85.group) do

          if value86.part and value86.part.Parent then

            local bodyPosition5 = value86.part:FindFirstChild("BodyPosition")

            local bodyGyro4 = value86.part:FindFirstChild("BodyGyro")

            if bodyPosition5 then
              bodyPosition5.Position = (val202.CFrame * value86.offset).Position
            end

            if bodyGyro4 then
              bodyGyro4.CFrame = val202.CFrame * value86.offset
            end
          end
        end

        mkGroupBillboard(val202, index31, #value85.group + 1)
        return
      end
    end

    return
  end

  function updateGroupDropdown()
    local val203 = {}

    for i6 = 1, #compiledGroups do
      table.insert(val203, "Group " .. i6)
    end

    if #val203 == 0 then
      val203 = { "No groups" }
      selectedGroupIndex = 1
    else
      selectedGroupIndex = math.clamp(selectedGroupIndex, 1, #compiledGroups)
    end

    pcall(function()

      if GroupDropdown then

        GroupDropdown:Refresh(val203)

        GroupDropdown:Set({ "Group " .. selectedGroupIndex })
      end

      return
    end)

    return
  end

  function compileGroup()
local val204

    if #anchoredParts == 0 then
      return
    else
      if #anchoredParts == 0 then
        return
      else
        val204 = nil
        local val205 = {}

        for index33, value87 in ipairs(anchoredParts) do

          if value87 and value87.Parent then
            if not val204 then
              val204 = value87
            else
              table.insert(val205, value87)
            end
          end
        end

        if not val204 then
          return
        else
          anchoredParts = { val204 }

          for index34, value88 in ipairs(val205) do
          end

          local anchored = val204.Anchored
          val204.Anchored = false

          if (val204:FindFirstChild("BodyPosition")) then

            val204.BodyPosition:Destroy()
          end

          if (val204:FindFirstChild("BodyGyro")) then

            val204.BodyGyro:Destroy()
          end

          local val206 = getHighlight(val204)

          if not val206 then
            local parent6 = val204.Parent
            local model2 = parent6

            if parent6 then

              model2 = val204.Parent:IsA("Model")
            end

            val206 = createHighlight(model2 and val204.Parent or val204)
          end

          val206.OutlineColor = Color3.new(0, 1, 0)
          local val207 = {}

          for index35, value89 in ipairs(anchoredParts) do
            local val208 = value89 ~= val204
            local parent7 = val208

            if val208 then

              parent7 = value89 and value89.Parent
            end

            if parent7 then
              local object6 = FindFirstAncestorOfType(value89, "Model")

              if object6 and object6 ~= workspace then
                for index36, value90 in ipairs(object6:GetDescendants()) do
                  if (value90:IsA("BasePart")) then
                    value90.CanCollide = false
                  end
                end
              else
                value89.CanCollide = false
              end

              local cframe2 = val204.CFrame

              table.insert(val207, {
                part = value89, offset = (cframe2:ToObjectSpace(value89.CFrame)), })
            end
          end

          local val209 = #compiledGroups + 1

          table.insert(compiledGroups, {
            primaryPart = val204, group = val207, wasAnchored = anchored, groupId = val209, })

          local cframe3 = val204:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CFrame")

          cframe3:Connect(function()
            updateBodyMovers(val204)
            return
          end)

          runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            updateBodyMovers(val204)
            return
          end)

          for index37, value91 in ipairs(val207) do
            local element15 = value91

            if element15.part and element15.part.Parent then
              local parent8 = element15.part.Parent
              local parent9 = parent8

              if parent8 then

                parent9 = (element15.part.Parent:IsA("Model")) and element15.part.Parent
              end

              local part = parent9 or element15.part

              part.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant3)
                onPartOwnerChanged(descendant3, element15.part)
                return
              end)
            end
          end

          mkGroupBillboard(val204, val209, #val207 + 1)
          iterate4(anchoredConnections)
          selectedGroupIndex = #compiledGroups
          updateGroupDropdown()
          return
        end
      end
    end
  end

  function deleteSelectedGroup()
    local val210 = #compiledGroups == 0
    local val211 = val210

    if not val210 then

      val211 = not selectedGroupIndex or selectedGroupIndex > #compiledGroups
    end

    if val211 then
      return
    else
      local element16 = compiledGroups[selectedGroupIndex]

      for index38, value92 in ipairs(element16.group) do

        if value92.part and value92.part.Parent then

          if (value92.part:FindFirstChild("BodyPosition")) then

            value92.part.BodyPosition:Destroy()
          end

          if (value92.part:FindFirstChild("BodyGyro")) then

            value92.part.BodyGyro:Destroy()
          end

          value92.part.CanCollide = true
          local target2 = getHighlight(value92.part)

          if target2 then
            target2:Destroy()
          end
        end
      end

      if element16.primaryPart and element16.primaryPart.Parent then

        if (element16.primaryPart:FindFirstChild("BodyPosition")) then

          element16.primaryPart.BodyPosition:Destroy()
        end

        if (element16.primaryPart:FindFirstChild("BodyGyro")) then

          element16.primaryPart.BodyGyro:Destroy()
        end

        element16.primaryPart.CanCollide = true
        local target3 = getHighlight(element16.primaryPart)

        if target3 then
          target3:Destroy()
        end

        if element16.wasAnchored then
          element16.primaryPart.Anchored = true
        end
      end

      rmGroupBillboard(element16.groupId)

      for index39, value93 in ipairs(compiledGroups) do
        value93.groupId = index39
      end

      if #compiledGroups > 0 then
        selectedGroupIndex = math.min(selectedGroupIndex, #compiledGroups)
      else
        selectedGroupIndex = 1
      end

      updateGroupDropdown()
      return
    end
  end

  function cleanupCompiledGroups()

    for index40, value94 in ipairs(compiledGroups) do
      for index41, value95 in ipairs(value94.group) do

        if value95.part and value95.part.Parent then

          if (value95.part:FindFirstChild("BodyPosition")) then

            value95.part.BodyPosition:Destroy()

            value95.part.BodyGyro:Destroy()

          value95.part.CanCollide = true

          if target4 then
            target4:Destroy()
          end
        end
      end

      if value94.primaryPart and value94.primaryPart.Parent then

        if (value94.primaryPart:FindFirstChild("BodyPosition")) then

          value94.primaryPart.BodyPosition:Destroy()

        if (value94.primaryPart:FindFirstChild("BodyGyro")) then

          target5:Destroy()

        if value94.wasAnchored then
          value94.primaryPart.Anchored = true

    iterate4(compileConnections)
    iterate4(renderSteppedConnections)

    groupBillboardGuis = {}
    partHighlights = {}
    selectedGroupIndex = 1
    updateGroupDropdown()
    return
  end

    function helper6()
      local currentCamera2 = workspace.CurrentCamera

      val36.i = userInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(p51)
        if not val36.on then
          return
        else
          if p51.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseWheel then
            if val36.d < 11 then
              val36.d = 11
            end

            if p51.Position.Z > 0 then
              local element17 = val36
              element17.d = element17.d + val36.inc
            else
              if p51.Position.Z < 0 then
                local element18 = val36
                element18.d = element18.d - val36.inc
              end
            end
          end

          return
        end
      end)

      val36.c = workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child10)
        local val212 = not val36.on
        local val213 = val212

        if not val212 then
          local val214 = child10.Name ~= "GrabParts"
          local val215 = val214

          if not val214 then
            val215 = not (child10:IsA("Model"))
          end

          val213 = val215
        end

        local dragPartScroll

        if val213 then
          return
        else
          task.wait(0.2)

          if not child10.Parent then
            return
          else
            local dragPart2 = child10:FindFirstChild("DragPart")

            if not dragPart2 then
              return
            else
              dragPartScroll = dragPart2:Clone()
              dragPartScroll.Name = "DragPart_scroll"

              local dragAttach = dragPartScroll:FindFirstChild("DragAttach")

              if (dragPartScroll:FindFirstChild("AlignPosition")) and dragAttach then
                dragPartScroll.AlignPosition.Attachment1 = dragAttach
              end

              dragPartScroll.Parent = child10
              val36.d = (dragPartScroll.Position - currentCamera2.CFrame.Position).Magnitude

              if (dragPartScroll:FindFirstChild("AlignOrientation")) then
                dragPartScroll.AlignOrientation.Enabled = false
              end

              if (dragPart2:FindFirstChild("AlignPosition")) then
                dragPart2.AlignPosition.Enabled = false
              end

              task.spawn(function()

                while true do

                  if child10.Parent and val36.on then
                    dragPartScroll.Position = currentCamera2.CFrame.Position
                      + currentCamera2.CFrame.LookVector * val36.d

                    runService.RenderStepped:Wait()
                  else
                    break
                  end
                end

                if dragPartScroll then
                  dragPartScroll:Destroy()
                end

                val36.d = 11
                return
              end)

              return
            end
          end
        end
      end)

      return
    end

    function helper7()

      if val36.i then

        val36.i:Disconnect()
        val36.i = nil
      end

      if val36.c then

        val36.c:Disconnect()
        val36.c = nil
      end

      val36.d = 11
      return
    end

    val36 = {
      d = 11, inc = 7, on = false, i = nil, c = nil, }

      return seat.Parent
    end
    local toys = workspace:FindFirstChild(val.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
    return toys and toys:FindFirstChild("CreatureBlobman") or nil
  end

  function modifyTarget(val233, val234, val235)

      end

      val234:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
      val234:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, false)
      val234:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)

        getPlayerFromCharacter.IsHeld.Value = false

        ragdolled4.Value = false

      bodyVelocity7.P = 1000000
      bodyVelocity7.Velocity = Vector3.new(math.random(-500, 50), -50, math.random(-50, 50))

      bodyAngularVelocity2.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(-10000000, -10000000, -10000000)
      bodyAngularVelocity2.P = 1000000

      bodyAngularVelocity2.AngularVelocity = Vector3.new(
        math.random(-500, 300), math.random(-300, 300), math.random(-500, 500)
      )

      bodyVelocity7.Parent = val233
      bodyAngularVelocity2.Parent = val233

      val234.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
      val234:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
      val234.RigType = Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15

      task.delay(2, function()
        if bodyVelocity7.Parent then
          bodyVelocity7:Destroy()

        if bodyAngularVelocity2.Parent then
          bodyAngularVelocity2:Destroy()
        end

        return
      end)

      return
    end
  end

  function performKill()

    if not w then
      return
    else
      local findFirstChild9 = players:FindFirstChild(w)
      local character43 = findFirstChild9 and findFirstChild9.Character

      local humanoidRootPart25 = character43 and character43:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

      local humanoid18 = character43 and character43:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

      local head5 = character43 and character43:FindFirstChild("Head")
      local val237 = findFirstChild9

      if findFirstChild9 then
        local val238 = humanoidRootPart25

        if humanoidRootPart25 then

          val238 = humanoid18 and head5
        end

        val237 = val238
      end

      if not val237 then
        return
      else
        if (isTooHigh(findFirstChild9)) then
          return
        else

          if (findFirstChild9:FindFirstChild("InPlot")) and findFirstChild9.InPlot.Value then
            return
          else
            if (humanoid18:GetState()) == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then
              return
            else
              local character44 = val.Character

              local humanoidRootPart26 = character44
                and character44:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

              if not (character44 and humanoidRootPart26) then
                return
              else
                if not (character44:GetAttribute("SavingOriginalPos")) then
                  saveOriginalPosAttr()
                end

                character44:SetAttribute("SavingOriginalPos", true)
                getgenv().originalFallenHeight = workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
                workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = (0 / 0)
                local val239 = getOriginalPosAttr()

                if val239 then
                  val41:attach(val239)
                end

                humanoidRootPart26:PivotTo((CFrame.new(humanoidRootPart25.Position + vector2)))
                iterate6(character43)
                setNetworkOwner:FireServer(humanoidRootPart25, humanoidRootPart25.CFrame)
                task.wait(0.05)
                destroyGrabLine:FireServer(humanoidRootPart25)
                task.wait(0.05)

                if (head5:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")) and head5.PartOwner.Value == val.Name then
                  task.wait(0.05)
                  modifyTarget(humanoidRootPart25, humanoid18, false)
                end

                scheduleReturnHome()
                return
              end
            end
          end
        end
      end
    end
  end

  function executeKick()

    if not w then
      return
    else
      local findFirstChild10 = players:FindFirstChild(w)
      local character45 = findFirstChild10 and findFirstChild10.Character

      local humanoidRootPart27 = character45 and character45:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

      local head6 = character45 and character45:FindFirstChild("Head")

      local humanoid19 = character45 and character45:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
      local val242 = humanoidRootPart27

      if humanoidRootPart27 then

        val242 = head6 and humanoid19
      end

      if not val242 or humanoid19.Health <= 0 then
        return
      else
        if (isTooHigh(findFirstChild10)) then
          return
        else

            return
          else
            local character46 = val.Character

            if not (character46 and character46:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")) then
              return
            else
              local getPivot = character46:GetPivot()
              character46:PivotTo(CFrame.new(humanoidRootPart27.Position + (Vector3.new(0, 0, -3))))
              iterate5(character45)
              setNetworkOwner:FireServer(humanoidRootPart27, humanoidRootPart27.CFrame)
              task.wait()
              character46:PivotTo(getPivot)
              task.wait(0.1)
              destroyGrabLine:FireServer(humanoidRootPart27)
              task.wait(0.1)
              local partOwner3 = head6:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")

              if partOwner3 and partOwner3.Value == val.Name then
                sendToSky(humanoidRootPart27, humanoid19)
              end

              return
            end
          end
        end
      end
    end
  end

                      task.wait(0.4)

                          end)

  LoopTab:CreateLabel("Utility", "settings")
  AutoFreezeBlobmanEnabled = false
  FreezedBlobs = {}
  trackedBlobs = {}

  function freezeBlobman(val277)

    local val278 = not val277 or not val277.PrimaryPart

    if val278 then
      return
    else
      if FreezedBlobs[val277] then
        return
      else
        FreezedBlobs[val277] = true

        local autoFreezeVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
        autoFreezeVelocity.Name = "AutoFreezeVelocity"
        autoFreezeVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
        autoFreezeVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero
        autoFreezeVelocity.P = 10000
        autoFreezeVelocity.Parent = val277.PrimaryPart

        local autoFreezeGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
        autoFreezeGyro.Name = "AutoFreezeGyro"
        autoFreezeGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
        autoFreezeGyro.P = 10000
        autoFreezeGyro.D = 500
        autoFreezeGyro.CFrame = val277.PrimaryPart.CFrame
        autoFreezeGyro.Parent = val277.PrimaryPart

        return
      end
    end
  end

  function unfreezeBlobman(val279)

    if not val279 or not val279.PrimaryPart then
      return
    else
      FreezedBlobs[val279] = nil

      pcall(function()

        local autoFreezeVelocity2 = val279.PrimaryPart:FindFirstChild("AutoFreezeVelocity")
        autoFreezeVelocity2:Destroy()

        val279.PrimaryPart:FindFirstChild("AutoFreezeGyro"):Destroy()
        return
      end)

      return
    end
  end

  function getPlayerSeatBlob()
    local character57 = val.Character

    local humanoid24 = character57 and character57:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local parent13 = humanoid24

    if humanoid24 then

      parent13 = humanoid24.SeatPart and humanoid24.SeatPart.Parent
    end

    return parent13
  end

  task.spawn(function()

    while true do
      task.wait(0.5)

      if AutoFreezeBlobmanEnabled then
        local val280 = getPlayerSeatBlob()

        for key11, value117 in pairs(trackedBlobs) do

          if key11 and key11.Parent then
            if key11 == val280 then
              freezeBlobman(key11)
            else
              unfreezeBlobman(key11)
            end
          else
            trackedBlobs[key11] = nil
          end
        end
      end
    end
  end)

  workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant4)

    if (descendant4:IsA("Model")) and descendant4.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
      trackedBlobs[descendant4] = true
    end

    return
  end)

  task.spawn(function()
    local object11 = workspace

    for index47, value118 in ipairs(object11:GetChildren()) do
      local val281 = value118:IsA("Folder")
      local spawnedInToys = val281

      if val281 then

        spawnedInToys = value118.Name:match("SpawnedInToys")
      end

      if spawnedInToys then
        for index48, value119 in ipairs(value118:GetDescendants()) do

          if (value119:IsA("Model")) and value119.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
            trackedBlobs[value119] = true
          end
        end
      end
    end

  function getPredictedCFrame(val390)
    local cframe18 = val390.CFrame

    if _G.PredictionTime <= 0 then
      return cframe18
    else
      local velocity = val390.Velocity
      local magnitude2 = velocity.Magnitude

      if magnitude2 < 1 then
        return cframe18
      else
        local val391 = val390.Position + velocity.Unit * (magnitude2 * _G.PredictionTime)
        return CFrame.new(val391, val391 + cframe18.LookVector)
      end
    end
  end

  function teleportBombToTarget(val392, val393)

  function setupBomb(val396)

    local val397 = not val396 or not val396.Parent

    if val397 then
      return false
    else
      if not val64 then
        return false
      else
        if not (val396:WaitForChild("ThisToysNumber", 2)) then
          cleanupBomb(val396, val63)
          return false
        else
          local val398 = 0

          while true do

            if not val396.PrimaryPart and val398 < 4 then
              task.wait(0.05)
              val398 = val398 + 0.05
            else
              break
            end
          end

          if not val396.PrimaryPart or not val396.Parent then
            cleanupBomb(val396, val63)
            return false
          else
            waitForChild12 = val396:WaitForChild("Body", 1)

            if not waitForChild12 then
              cleanupBomb(val396, val63)
              return false
            else

              if not (pcall(function()
                setNetworkOwner:FireServer(waitForChild12, waitForChild12.CFrame)
                local waitForChild13 = waitForChild12:WaitForChild("PartOwner", 0.5)

                if not waitForChild13 or waitForChild13.Value ~= val.Name then
                  return false
                else
                  local val399 = val4
                  local head11 = val399

                  if val399 then

                    head11 = val4:FindFirstChild("Head")
                  end

                  if head11 then
                    local val400 = #val62
                    local floorVal = val4.Head.Position + (Vector3.new(0, 1000, 0)) + (Vector3.new(val400 % 8 * 6, -(math.floor(val400 / 8)) * 6, 0))

                    for index64, value195 in ipairs(val396:GetChildren()) do
                      if (value195:IsA("BasePart")) then
                        value195.CanCollide = false
                      end
                    end

                    val396:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(CFrame.new(floorVal))
                    task.wait(0.2)

                    for index65, value196 in ipairs(val396:GetChildren()) do
                      if (value196:IsA("BasePart")) then
                        value196.Anchored = true
                      end
                    end
                  end

                  return
                end
              end)) or not val396.Parent then
                cleanupBomb(val396, val63)
                return false
              else
                table.insert(val62, val396)
                cleanupBomb(val396, val63)

                val396.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
                  if not val396.Parent then
                    cleanupBomb(val396, val62)
                  end

                  return
                end)

                return true
              end
            end
          end
        end
      end
    end
  end

  function spawnBombs()

    if val66 then
      return
    else
      local val402 = not val4

          return

          if val404 <= 0 then
            return

            val66 = true

            vector8 = Vector3.new(0, 90, 0)

  end

  function explodeOnTarget(val405)

    if val67 then
      return false
    else

      if not val405 or not val405.Character then
        return false
      else

        if not (val405.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")) then
          return false
        else
          local val406 = {}

          for index67, value198 in ipairs(val62) do
            if (isValidBomb(value198)) then
              table.insert(val406, value198)
            end
          end

          if #val406 < _G.MaxMissiles then
            return false
          else
            local val407 = {}
            val67 = true

            for i13 = 1, _G.MaxMissiles do
              val407[i13] = val406[i13]
            end

            val62 = {}

            for index68, value199 in ipairs(val407) do
              if not val405.Character then
                break
              else

                if not humanoidRootPart61 or not humanoidRootPart61.Parent then
                  break

                    teleportBombToTarget(value199, humanoidRootPart61)

                    local val409 = {
                      {
                        Radius = 17.5, TimeLength = 2, Hitbox = (value199:FindFirstChild("PartHitDetector")), ExplodesByFire = false, MaxForcePerStudSquared = 225, Model = value199, ImpactSpeed = 100, ExplodesByPointy = false, DestroysModel = false, PositionPart = humanoidRootPart61, }, humanoidRootPart61.Position, }

                    if (pcall(function()
                      bombExplode:FireServer(unpack(val409))
                      return
                    end)) then
                      val408 = val408 + 1
                    end
                  end

                  if _G.ExplosionMode == "Sequential" then
                    task.wait(_G.ExplosionDelay)
                  else
                    task.wait(0.01)
                  end
                end
              end
            end

            task.wait(0.1)

            return val408 > 0
          end
        end
      end
    end
  end

  function autoSpamLoop()

    while val64 do

      local findFirstChild20 = val410

      if val410 then
        findFirstChild20 = players:FindFirstChild(val65)
      end

      local element43 = findFirstChild20

      if element43 and element43.Character then

          if not val66 and not val67 then
            spawnBombs()
            task.wait(0.3)

            if #val62 >= 1 then
              explodeOnTarget(element43)
            end
          end
        else

          if #val62 < _G.MaxMissiles and not val66 then
            spawnBombs()
          end

          if #val62 >= _G.MaxMissiles and not val67 then
            if (explodeOnTarget(element43)) then
              task.wait(0.4)
            end
          end
        end
      end

      task.wait(0.1)
    end

    return
  end

  function checkObjectInRaycast()
    local currentCamera7 = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local position5 = currentCamera7.CFrame.Position

    local raycastParams = RaycastParams.new()

    raycastParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Blacklist
    raycastParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { val.Character }

    local raycast = workspace:Raycast(
      position5, currentCamera7.CFrame.LookVector * 30, raycastParams
    )

    if raycast then
      local instance2 = raycast.Instance

      if (instance2:IsA("BasePart")) and not (isPlayerCharacter(instance2)) then
        return true, (position5 - raycast.Position).Magnitude
      else
        
        return false, math.huge
      end
    else
      return false, math.huge
    end
  end

  function CleanupGrab()

      currentGrabbedPart = nil
    end

    if connect28 then

      connect28:Disconnect()
      connect28 = nil
    end

    if connect29 then

      connect29 = nil

    userInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default

    pcall(function()

      val.PlayerScripts.CharacterAndBeamMove.GrabNotifyEvent:Fire(false)
      return
    end)

    return
  end

  end

  function lookAt(val451, val452)
    local unit = (val452 - val451).Unit
    local cross = unit:Cross(Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))
    local cross2 = cross:Cross(unit)
    return CFrame.fromMatrix(val451, cross, cross2)
  end

  function CBM(val470)

    local wingGyro2 = wingGyro or Instance.new("BodyGyro")

    local wingPos2 = wingPos or Instance.new("BodyPosition")
    wingGyro2.Name = "WingGyro"
    wingPos2.Name = "WingPos"

    wingGyro2.P = 15000
    wingGyro2.D = 200
    wingGyro2.MaxTorque = (Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)) * 10000000000

    wingPos2.P = 15000
    wingPos2.D = 200
    wingPos2.MaxForce = (Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)) * 10000000000

    wingGyro2.Parent = val470
    wingPos2.Parent = val470
    return wingGyro2, wingPos2
  end

  function generateShapePath()

    if val87 then
      return
    else
      val86 = {}
      local val511 = val80

      if val79 == "Heart" then
        for i24 = 0, 3000 do
          local val512 = i24 / 3000 * math.pi * 2
          local val513 = math.sin(val512)
          local val514 = math.cos(val512)
          local val515 = math.cos(2 * val512)
          local val516 = math.cos(3 * val512)
          local val517 = math.cos(4 * val512)

            16 * val513 * val513 * val513 * val511, (13 * val514 - 5 * val515 - 2 * val516 - val517) * val511 + 2, 0

                      + (Vector3.new(0, 2, 0))

  function getSmoothedShapePos(val552)

    if #val86 == 0 then
      return Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
    else
      local val553 = val552 * #val86
      local floorVal5 = (math.floor(val553)) % #val86

      return val86[floorVal5]:Lerp(val86[(floorVal5 + 1) % #val86], val553 - (math.floor(val553)))
    end
  end

  end

  function SE_CBM(val554)

    heartGyro2.P = 15000
    heartGyro2.D = 200

    heartPos2.P = 15000
    heartPos2.D = 200

    heartGyro2.MaxTorque = (Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)) * 10000000000
    heartPos2.MaxForce = (Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)) * 10000000000
    heartGyro2.Parent = val554
    heartPos2.Parent = val554

  end

  function toy_spawn(val584, val585, val586)
    local val587 = (typeof(val586)) == "string"
    local val588 = val587
    val588 = val587 and (string.lower(val586)) == "headup"

    local val589 = val588
        and (CFrame.new(val585.Position + (Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)), val585.Position))
          * (CFrame.Angles(math.pi, 0, 0))
      or val585

    task.spawn(function()

      local zero = (typeof(val586)) == "Vector3" and val586 or Vector3.zero
      SpawnToyRF:InvokeServer(val584, val589, zero)
      return
    end)

    local val590 = tick()

    local val591

    local connect34 = waitForChild.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child14)
      if child14.Name == val584 then
        val591 = child14
      end

      return
    end)

    if not val591 then
      tick()
    end

    local val592

    repeat
      task.wait()

      val592 = val591 or (tick()) - val590 > 5
    until val592

    connect34:Disconnect()
    return val591
  end

  function helper13()

      val601 = scriptRagdollToy or isRespawning
    end

    else

      local generateGUID2 = HttpService:GenerateGUID(false)

      for i37 = 1, 5 do

        if character91 then

          head14 = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
        end

          isRespawning = false
          return

          if ragdollPallet then

            scriptRagdollID2.Name = "ScriptRagdollID"
            scriptRagdollID2.Value = generateGUID2
            scriptRagdollID2.Parent = ragdollPallet

            ragdollPallet.Name = "RagdollPallet"
            local waitForChild21 = ragdollPallet:WaitForChild("SoundPart", 5)

            if waitForChild21 then
              task.spawn(function()

                while true do
                  local val603 = formEnabled
                  local parent20 = val603

                  if val603 then

                    parent20 = waitForChild21 and waitForChild21.Parent
                  end

                  if parent20 then

                    if not (waitForChild21:FindFirstChild("PartOwner"))
                      or waitForChild21.PartOwner.Value ~= player.Name then
                      setNetworkOwner:FireServer(waitForChild21, waitForChild21.CFrame)
                    end

                    task.wait(0.05)
                  else
                    break
                  end
                end

                return
              end)

              local instance3 = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", waitForChild21)
              instance3.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0)
              instance3.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 1000, 0)

              for key54, value276 in pairs(ragdollPallet:GetChildren()) do
                if (value276:IsA("BasePart")) then
                  value276.Transparency = 1
                  value276.Size = Vector3.new(0.5, 0.5, 0.5)
                  value276.CanCollide = false
                end
              end

              if (waitForChild21:FindFirstChild("Wood")) then

                waitForChild21.Wood:Destroy()
              end

              scriptRagdollToy = ragdollPallet
              RAGDOLL_UNIQUE_ID = generateGUID2
              isRespawning = false
              UpdateRagdollPalletMenu()
              return
            else
              
              task.wait(0.3)
            end
          else
            task.wait(0.3)
          end
        end
      end

  end

  function DestroyRagdoll()

    scriptRagdollToy = nil
    return
  end

    if ragdollMonitor then

    end

    ragdollMonitor = task.spawn(function()

              or scriptRagdollToy.ScriptRagdollID.Value ~= RAGDOLL_UNIQUE_ID
          end

          val605 = scriptRagdollID4
        end

        if val605 then
          scriptRagdollToy = nil
          helper13()
        end
      end

      return
    end)

    return
  end

  function GetGrabbedCharacter()

    local grabParts8 = workspace:FindFirstChild("GrabParts")

    if not grabParts8 then
      return nil
    else
      local grabPart11 = grabParts8:FindFirstChild("GrabPart")

      if not grabPart11 then
        return nil
      else
        local weldConstraint10 = grabPart11:FindFirstChildOfClass("WeldConstraint")

        local weld4 = weldConstraint10 or grabPart11:FindFirstChild("Weld")
        local val608 = weld4

        if weld4 then
          local part15 = weld4.Part1
          local model7 = part15

          if part15 then

            model7 = weld4.Part1:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
          end

          val608 = model7
        end

        return val608
      end
    end
  end

  function RestoreCollisions(val609)

    if not val609 then
      return
    else

      if val609.torso and val609.torso.Parent then
        val609.torso.CanCollide = true
      end

      if val609.head and val609.head.Parent then
        val609.head.CanCollide = true
      end

      if val609.hrp and val609.hrp.Parent then
        val609.hrp.CanCollide = true
      end

      for index110, value277 in ipairs(val609.limbs) do

        if value277.part and value277.part.Parent then
          value277.part.CanCollide = true
        end
      end

      return
    end
  end

  function ApplyNoclipToForm(val610)

    local parent22 = val610.torso and val610.torso.Parent

    if parent22 then
      val610.torso.CanCollide = false
    end

    if val610.head and val610.head.Parent then
      val610.head.CanCollide = false
    end

    if val610.hrp and val610.hrp.Parent then
      val610.hrp.CanCollide = false
    end

    for index111, value278 in ipairs(val610.limbs) do

      if value278.part and value278.part.Parent then
        value278.part.CanCollide = false
      end
    end

    return
  end

  function GetCharacterParts(val611)
    if not val611 then
      return nil
    else
      local torso4 = val611:FindFirstChild("Torso")

      if not torso4 then
        return nil
      else
        local val612 = {}

        for index112, value279 in ipairs({ "Left Arm", "Right Arm", "Left Leg", "Right Leg" }) do
          local findFirstChild25 = val611:FindFirstChild(value279)

          if findFirstChild25 then
            local ragdollLimbPart = findFirstChild25:FindFirstChild("RagdollLimbPart")

            if ragdollLimbPart and ragdollLimbPart:IsA("BasePart") then
              table.insert(val612, { part = ragdollLimbPart, name = value279 })
            end
          end
        end

        return {
          torso = torso4, head = (val611:FindFirstChild("Head")), hrp = (val611:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")), limbs = val612, }
      end
    end
  end

  function helper14()

    local val613 = not activeForm or not formEnabled

    if val613 then
      return
    else

      local head15 = activeForm.char:FindFirstChild("Head")
      local val614 = not head15

      if val614 or not (head15:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")) then
        return
      else
        local parts = activeForm.parts
        local character92 = player.Character

        if not character92 then
          return
        else
          local torso5 = character92:FindFirstChild("Torso")

          local humanoidRootPart75 = torso5 or character92:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
          local val615 = not humanoidRootPart75
          local val616 = val615

          if not val615 then

            val616 = not parts.torso or not parts.torso.Parent
          end

          if val616 then
            return
          else

            if parts.hrp and parts.hrp.Parent then
              parts.hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0)
              parts.hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
              parts.hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end

            local unit2 = Vector3.new(
              humanoidRootPart75.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, humanoidRootPart75.CFrame.LookVector.Z
            ).Unit

            local val617 = (Vector3.new(
              humanoidRootPart75.Position.X, humanoidRootPart75.Position.Y, humanoidRootPart75.Position.Z
            )) + unit2 * formDistance

            local val618 = (CFrame.new(val617, val617 + unit2))
              * (CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(formOrientation), 0))

            local element56 = formPresets[currentPreset]
            ApplyNoclipToForm(parts)

            parts.torso.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            parts.torso.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            parts.torso.Anchored = false
            parts.torso.CFrame = val618 * (CFrame.new(element56.Torso.pos)) * element56.Torso.rot

            if parts.head and parts.head.Parent then
              parts.head.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
              parts.head.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
              parts.head.Anchored = false
              parts.head.CFrame = val618 * (CFrame.new(element56.Head.pos)) * element56.Head.rot
            end

            for index113, value280 in ipairs(parts.limbs) do

              if value280.part and value280.part.Parent then
                value280.part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                value280.part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                value280.part.Anchored = false

                local element57 = element56[value280.name]

                if element57 then
                  value280.part.CFrame = val618 * (CFrame.new(element57.pos)) * element57.rot
                end
              end
            end

            return
          end
        end
      end
    end
  end

  function SetupForm(val619)

    if not formEnabled then
      return
    else
      humanoid31 = val619:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

      if not humanoid31 then
        return
      else
        task.wait(0.3)
        local val620 = GetCharacterParts(val619)

        if not val620 then
          return
        else
          activeForm = {
            char = val619, parts = val620, isGrabbed = true, connection = nil, }

          task.spawn(function()

            while true do
              local val621 = activeForm
              local val622 = val621

              if val621 then
                local val623 = activeForm.char == val619
                local val624 = val623

                if val623 then

                  val624 = val619.Parent and formEnabled
                end

                val622 = val624
              end

              if val622 then
                local head16 = val619:FindFirstChild("Head")
                local partOwner8 = head16

                if head16 then

                  partOwner8 = (head16:FindFirstChild("PartOwner"))
                    and head16.PartOwner.Value == player.Name
                end

                if not partOwner8 then
                  local character93 = player.Character
                  local humanoidRootPart76 = character93

                  if character93 then

                    humanoidRootPart76 = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                  end

                  local element58 = humanoidRootPart76
                  local val625 = head16

                  if head16 then

                    val625 = element58 and (element58.Position - head16.Position).Magnitude <= 60
                  end

                  if val625 then
                    setNetworkOwner:FireServer(head16, head16.CFrame)
                  end
                end

                local ragdolled5 = humanoid31:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
                local val626 = ragdolled5

                if ragdolled5 then

                  val626 = not ragdolled5.Value and scriptRagdollToy
                end

                if val626 then

                  local soundPart7 = scriptRagdollToy:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")

                  if soundPart7 and head16 then
                    firetouchinterest(soundPart7, head16, 0)
                    task.wait()
                    firetouchinterest(soundPart7, head16, 1)
                  end
                end

                task.wait(0.1)
              else
                break
              end
            end

            return
          end)

          activeForm.connection = runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()

            if not formEnabled then
              return
            else

              activeForm.isGrabbed = (GetGrabbedCharacter()) == val619
              helper14()
              return
            end
          end)

          return
        end
      end
    end
  end

  function StopCurrentForm()

    if activeForm then
      if activeForm.parts then
        RestoreCollisions(activeForm.parts)
      end

      if activeForm.connection then

        activeForm.connection:Disconnect()
      end
    end

    activeForm = nil
    return
  end

  FormTab:CreateParagraph({
    Title = "NOTE", Content = "DO   <font face=\"GothamBlack\" color=\"rgb(255,0,0)\">NOT ENABLE</font>   Form Grab in House.  Form Grab Work on <font face=\"GothamBlack\" color=\"rgb(255,0,0)\">NPC</font> and <font face=\"GothamBlack\" color=\"rgb(255,0,0)\">PLAYER</font>", })

  task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(0.1) do
      if formEnabled then
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
          local grabbed = GetGrabbedCharacter()
          if grabbed and not activeForm then SetupForm(grabbed) end
          if not activeForm then return end
          local target = activeForm.char
          local function drop(reason)
            rayfield:Notify({Title = "ERROR", Image = "x", Content = reason, Duration = 3})
            StopCurrentForm()
          end
          if not target or not target.Parent then drop("Target Lost"); return end
          local character = player.Character
          local head = target:FindFirstChild("Head")
          local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
          if hrp and head and (hrp.Position - head.Position).Magnitude > 30 then
            drop("Target exceeded 30 studs - Drop")
            return
          end
          local targetPlayer = players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(target)
          if not targetPlayer then return end
          local states = workspace:FindFirstChild("Players")
          local state = states and states:FindFirstChild(targetPlayer.Name)
          local plot = state and state:FindFirstChild("InPlot")
          local playerPlot = targetPlayer:FindFirstChild("InPlot")
          if (plot and plot.Value == true) or (playerPlot and playerPlot.Value == true) then
            drop("Target in plot - Drop")
          end
        end)
        if not ok then
          warn("Form monitor: " .. tostring(err))
          formEnabled = false
          pcall(StopCurrentForm)
        end
      end
    end
  end)

  player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()

    local connection = activeForm and activeForm.connection

    if connection then

      activeForm.connection:Disconnect()
    end

    activeForm = nil
    return
  end)

  _G.Bloody = true
  return
end
